#1 – Lvl 6 Demonic – Arctic/Far North Settlement
Template: Skirmish Line
Battlemap: Arctic/Far North Settlement

Monster                     Role          Rank       Lvl  Qty  Source
---------------------------------------------------------------------
Sahuagin Raider             Soldier       Standard     6    1  Monster Manual 1 p.224
Tiefling Heretic            Artillery     Standard     6    1  Monster Manual 1 p.250
Bugbear Strangler (Goblin)  Lurker        Standard     6    1  Monster Manual 1 p.136
Shadar-Kai Witch            Controller    Standard     7    1  Monster Manual 1 p.231

Mini Figures:
  Sahuagin Raider (×1) → Darkray (×1 of 3 available)
  Tiefling Heretic (×1) → Odi’ram, Lord of Rage (×1 of 1 available)
  Bugbear Strangler (Goblin) (×1) → The Head Hunter (×1 of 1 available)
  Shadar-Kai Witch (×1) → Zendel Coldheart, the Ice Queen (×1 of 1 available)

Terrain: Frozen Lake
Cracked ice over dark water. The surface is slippery and sections may break under weight.

Terrain Powers:
  Cracking Ice
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: A 3×3 square section cracks. Creatures in the area must succeed on a DC 14 Acrobatics check or fall prone. On the next round, the section breaks — creatures still there fall into freezing water (2d6 cold damage, restrained until escape DC 15).
    Recharge: Each section can only crack once
  Slippery Surface
    Trigger: A creature charges or runs on the ice
    Effect: The creature must succeed on a DC 12 Acrobatics check or slide 1d4 squares in its direction of movement past its target.

Terrain: Choke Frost
Light, white mist that congeals into thick ice as creatures move through it, slowing and immobilizing. (DMG p.67)

Terrain Powers:
  Frost Snap
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: Choke frost congeals. Each creature in a frost square is slowed (save ends). A creature already slowed is immobilized instead (save ends).
  Ice Tomb
    Trigger: A creature ends its turn immobilized in choke frost
    Effect: The creature is restrained (save ends). On a failed save, the creature is instead stunned (save ends).
    Recharge: Single-use per creature per encounter

Battlemap: Hell/Lava Lands

Monster                     Role          Rank       Lvl  Qty  Source
---------------------------------------------------------------------

Magma Hurler (Magma Beast)  Artillery     Standard     4    1  Monster Manual 1 p.182
Ambush Drake                Skirmisher    Standard     6    1  Draconomicon 1 p.222
Blizzard Dragon Wyrmling    Controller    Standard     2    1  Monster Manual 3 p.65

Mini Figures:
  Rage Drake (×1) → Alphariox, Dragon Forebearer (×1 of 1 available)
  Magma Hurler (Magma Beast) (×1) → Lava Horror (×1 of 1 available)
  Ambush Drake (×1) → Eyegouger Guardian Drake (×1 of 1 available)
  Blizzard Dragon Wyrmling (×1) → Glutandur, Venom Dragon (×1 of 1 available)

Terrain: Chaos Spots
Certain locations in constant flux — terrain shifts, gravity reverses, and the environment changes unpredictably. (The Plane Above p.109)

Terrain Powers:
  Reality Hiccup
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: GM rolls 1d6: 1-2=gravity reverses for 1 round, 3-4=all creatures shift 1 square clockwise, 5-6=terrain type in a burst 3 changes (water, fire, ice).
  Reality Inversion
    Trigger: A creature uses a healing power in a chaos spot
    Effect: The chaos spot inverts the healing. The healing is halved, and one random enemy within 5 squares heals for the amount lost. If no enemy is in range, the healing works normally.

Terrain: Lava Bridge
A narrow stone crossing over lava channels, with dangerous edges and heat shimmer.

Terrain Powers:
  Erupting Fissure
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: A 2×2 square section of the bridge cracks. Each creature in the area takes 2d6 fire damage and is pushed 1 square toward the nearest edge. The squares become difficult terrain.
    Recharge: Recharges when no creature is in the area
  Heat Shimmer
    Trigger: A creature ends its turn on the bridge
    Effect: The creature grants combat advantage until the start of its next turn as heat distortion warps line of sight.

  Needlefang Drake Swarm (×1) → Pack of Rats (×1 of 1 available)
  Spitting Drake (×1) → Dilophosaurus (×1 of 1 available)
  Dragonborn Sellsword (×1) → Alexandra Uthgarde (×1 of 3 available)

Terrain: Godrock
Where fragments of Torog's bones struck the earth, formations called godrock sprouted. They radiate lingering divine agony. (Underdark p.119)

Terrain Powers:
  Agony Pulse
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: Each creature within burst 2 of the godrock takes 1d6 psychic damage and is slowed until end of its next turn (DC 15 Will negates the slowed condition).
  Echo of Agony
    Trigger: A creature is reduced to bloodied while touching godrock
    Effect: The godrock channels Torog's suffering. Each creature within 3 squares takes 1d8 psychic damage and is immobilized until the end of its next turn.

Terrain: Celestial Ruins
Shattered marble halls glowing with fading divine radiance. Holy ground shifts allegiance.

Terrain Powers:
  Divine Radiance Flare
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: Fading divine energy pulses from a ruin fragment. Each undead or fiend creature within burst 3 takes 1d10 radiant damage. Each other creature in the area gains 5 temporary hit points.
  Shifting Allegiance
    Trigger: A creature with a healing power uses it in the ruins
    Effect: Holy ground resonates — the healing grants 1d6 additional hit points.

Mini Figures:
  Rage Drake (×1) → Alphariox, Dragon Forebearer (×1 of 1 available)
  Spitting Drake (×1) → Dilophosaurus (×1 of 1 available)
  Dragonborn Sellsword (×1) → Alexandra Uthgarde (×1 of 3 available)
  Blizzard Dragon Wyrmling (×1) → Glutandur, Venom Dragon (×1 of 1 available)

Terrain: Dead Magic
The most powerful arcane magic completely drains an area of plant and animal life, leaving a dead magic zone. (Dark Sun Creature Catalog p.35)

Terrain Powers:
  Power Void
    Trigger: A creature enters the dead magic zone
    Effect: Encounter and daily powers cannot be used in the zone. At-will powers function normally. Magic items provide no bonuses.
  Magic Backlash
    Trigger: A creature attempts to use a power with the Arcane keyword in dead magic terrain
    Effect: The power functions at half effect (half damage, half duration). The caster takes 5 psychic damage from the strain of forcing magic through dead terrain.

Terrain: Carpet of Serpents
Swarms of serpents or other venomous creatures that dart in and out of hidden dens, striking exposed ankles. (The Plane Above p.86)

Terrain Powers:
  Serpent Strike
    Trigger: A creature enters a serpent square
    Effect: 1d8 poison damage and ongoing 5 poison damage (save ends). The creature is slowed as serpents coil around its legs.
  Serpent Tangle
    Trigger: A creature falls prone in the carpet of serpents
    Effect: Serpents swarm over the creature. It is restrained (save ends) and takes ongoing 5 poison damage (save ends both). While restrained, the creature takes a −2 penalty to attack rolls.

  Dire Boar (×1) → Beast (×1 of 2 available)
  Faerie Dragon Windgleam, Adult (×1) → Glutandur, Venom Dragon (×1 of 1 available)

Terrain: Armorfields
Where Moradin's exalted tread, armorfields spring up — ground that hardens and protects those who stand upon it. (The Plane Above p.50)

Terrain Powers:
  Armored Ground
    Trigger: A creature starts its turn on armorfields
    Effect: The creature gains +1 to AC and Fortitude until end of its turn.
  Protective Surge
    Trigger: A creature on armorfields is hit by a critical hit
    Effect: The armorfields absorb part of the blow. The critical hit is downgraded to a normal hit, and the creature gains +2 to AC until the end of its next turn.
    Recharge: Recharges when no creature on the armorfields is bloodied

Terrain: Energy Node
A particularly strong confluence of arcane, divine, or other forms of power. Boosts attacks or healing for those standing within. (DMG2 p.59)

Terrain Powers:
  Energy Surge
    Trigger: A creature uses a power with a damage roll while in the node
    Effect: Add 1d6 extra damage of a random type (roll 1d4: 1=fire, 2=cold, 3=lightning, 4=thunder).
  Node Overcharge
    Trigger: Three or more creatures occupy squares adjacent to the energy node simultaneously
    Effect: The node discharges. Close burst 3; each creature in the burst takes 2d8 force damage and is pushed 2 squares. The node becomes inert for 1 round.

  Web Collapse
    Trigger: A creature uses a fire attack in or adjacent to spiderwebs
    Effect: The webs in a burst 2 centered on the target square burn away (no longer difficult terrain), and each creature in the burst takes 1d6 fire damage. Any creature restrained by webs is freed.

  Bugbear Warrior (Goblin) (×1) → Bugbear Gladiator (×1 of 3 available)
  Bugbear Strangler (Goblin) (×1) → Goblin Lancer (×1 of 2 available)

Terrain: Defiled Terrain
Terrain created by the use of powerful defiling magic — withered, lifeless ground that saps vitality. (Dark Sun Creature Catalog p.34)

Terrain Powers:
  Withering Touch
    Trigger: A creature starts its turn on defiled terrain
    Effect: The creature takes 5 necrotic damage and cannot regain HP by any means until end of its next turn.
  Ash Revenant
    Trigger: A creature dies on defiled terrain
    Effect: At the end of the round, the corpse crumbles to ash and an ash wraith minion rises in its square. The wraith acts on initiative count 0 and attacks the nearest living creature. It lasts until destroyed or until the end of the encounter.
    Recharge: Single-use per encounter

Terrain: Grasping Vines
Vines, roots, and vegetation infused with aberrant energies that entangle those who come near. (The Plane Above p.107)

Terrain Powers:
  Vine Snare
    Trigger: A creature enters or starts its turn in vine squares
    Effect: The creature is restrained (escape DC 14 Athletics). While restrained, the vines deal 1d6 damage at the start of each turn.
  Vine Drag
    Trigger: A creature fails its first save against vine snare's restrained condition
    Effect: The vines pull the creature 1 square deeper into the vine patch and the creature takes 5 damage. Each subsequent failed save moves the creature 1 additional square.

Terrain: The Weeping Lady
The heart-wrenching sobs of a seemingly female voice haunt the Deeps, distracting and demoralizing those who hear her. (Underdark p.64)

Terrain Powers:
  Sorrowful Wail
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: Each creature in the area must succeed on DC 15 Will or be immobilized until end of its next turn as grief overwhelms it. Creatures that save are slowed instead.
  Despairing Grasp
    Trigger: A creature fails a saving throw against the Weeping Lady's wail
    Effect: The creature is immobilized (save ends) as spectral hands rise from the ground to hold it. While immobilized, the creature takes a −2 penalty to Will defense.

Terrain: Aberrant Structures
Desiccated, long-dead structures in ruins left over from Nihilath. Their alien geometry warps perception. (Underdark p.82)

Terrain Powers:
  Warped Geometry
    Trigger: A creature uses a ranged power while within the structures
    Effect: The range changes by 1d4−2 squares (may increase or decrease). On a result of 0 or less, the attack targets a random creature within range instead.
  Alien Geometry
    Trigger: A creature attempts to move more than 4 squares in a single turn within the structures
    Effect: The creature must succeed on a Dungeoneering check (moderate DC) or its movement is redirected to a random adjacent square and the creature is dazed until the end of its next turn.

Terrain: Crystal Cavern
Razor-sharp crystal formations refract light into blinding patterns and create echoes.

Terrain Powers:
  Prismatic Flash
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: Crystals refract ambient energy. Each creature in the cavern must succeed on DC 14 Fortitude or be blinded until end of its next turn.
  Resonance Shatter
    Trigger: A thunder or force power hits a crystal formation
    Effect: The crystal explodes. Each creature within burst 1 of the crystal takes 2d6 damage. The crystal is destroyed.

Terrain: Primal Mud
Organic sludge that forms where life force rises up through the Feydark into the Feywild. Teeming with nascent life. (Underdark p.101)

Terrain Powers:
  Life Surge
    Trigger: A creature falls prone in primal mud
    Effect: Tiny creatures sprout and attack: the prone creature takes 1d8 damage and is dazed until it stands. If the creature is bloodied, it regains 5 HP as the mud's primal energy responds to weakness.
  Regenerative Mire
    Trigger: A bloodied creature is prone in primal mud at the start of its turn
    Effect: The creature regains hit points equal to its level. Undead and constructs instead sink deeper — they are slowed (save ends).

Terrain: Doomlight Crystal
Glowing rock formations trapping volatile gas that explodes violently on contact with air. (DMG2 p.59)

Terrain Powers:
  Volatile Detonation
    Trigger: A doomlight crystal takes any damage
    Effect: It explodes: burst 2, 2d8 damage to each creature in the area (DC 15 Reflex for half). Crystal is destroyed.
  Sympathetic Resonance
    Trigger: A doomlight crystal detonates
    Effect: Each other doomlight crystal within 5 squares pulses. Creatures adjacent to any pulsing crystal take 1d6 radiant damage and are dazed until the end of their next turn.

Terrain: Needle Hedge
Sharp-needled bushes from the dark forests of the Shadowfell that slash at creatures passing through. (DMG2 p.61)

Terrain Powers:
  Thorn Slash
    Trigger: A creature is pushed or pulled through a needle hedge square
    Effect: The creature takes 2d6 damage and ongoing 5 damage (save ends) from embedded thorns.
  Thorny Retaliation
    Trigger: A creature forces another creature through needle hedge squares
    Effect: The forced creature takes 1d6 + half level damage per square of needle hedge moved through, and ongoing 5 poison damage (save ends).

Terrain: Clifftop Camp
A campsite on a rocky ledge. One side drops away steeply — forced movement is deadly.

Terrain Powers:
  Cliff Edge
    Trigger: A creature is pushed to within 1 square of the cliff edge
    Effect: DC 15 Acrobatics or the creature goes over. It can grab the edge (DC 12 Athletics) or fall, taking 5d10 damage.
  Loose Stones
    Trigger: A creature within 2 squares of the cliff edge is hit by a push or slide effect
    Effect: The forced movement increases by 1 square. If this forces the creature over the edge, the creature can make a saving throw to catch the ledge instead of falling.

Terrain: Loadstone
This strange rock dramatically increases the weight of all objects near it, slowing movement and hampering flight. (DMG p.68)

Terrain Powers:
  Gravitational Pull
    Trigger: A creature starts its turn within 2 squares of the loadstone
    Effect: The creature is pulled 1 square toward the loadstone and is slowed until end of its turn. Flying creatures are pulled 2 squares instead and must land if pulled adjacent.
  Crushing Weight
    Trigger: A creature in heavy armor starts its turn adjacent to loadstone
    Effect: The creature is slowed until the end of its next turn and takes a −2 penalty to Reflex defense. Creatures in light or no armor are unaffected.

Terrain: Grasping Slime
Black, viscous goo whose clingy surface can cause creatures to become stuck in place. (DMG p.68)

Terrain Powers:
  Sticky Grasp
    Trigger: A creature enters a grasping slime square
    Effect: The creature is immobilized (escape DC 15 Athletics). A creature that starts its turn immobilized by the slime takes 1d6 acid damage.
  Slime Coat
    Trigger: A creature starts its turn restrained by grasping slime
    Effect: The creature takes ongoing 5 acid damage (save ends). While taking this ongoing damage, the creature grants combat advantage.

Terrain: Grasping Vines
Vines, roots, and vegetation infused with aberrant energies that entangle those who come near. (The Plane Above p.107)

Terrain Powers:
  Vine Snare
    Trigger: A creature enters or starts its turn in vine squares
    Effect: The creature is restrained (escape DC 14 Athletics). While restrained, the vines deal 1d6 damage at the start of each turn.
  Vine Drag
    Trigger: A creature fails its first save against vine snare's restrained condition
    Effect: The vines pull the creature 1 square deeper into the vine patch and the creature takes 5 damage. Each subsequent failed save moves the creature 1 additional square.

Terrain: Frozen Lake
Cracked ice over dark water. The surface is slippery and sections may break under weight.

Terrain Powers:
  Cracking Ice
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: A 3×3 square section cracks. Creatures in the area must succeed on a DC 14 Acrobatics check or fall prone. On the next round, the section breaks — creatures still there fall into freezing water (2d6 cold damage, restrained until escape DC 15).
    Recharge: Each section can only crack once
  Slippery Surface
    Trigger: A creature charges or runs on the ice
    Effect: The creature must succeed on a DC 12 Acrobatics check or slide 1d4 squares in its direction of movement past its target.

Terrain: Frozen Fire
Portions of the Elemental Chaos experience cold so intense that even fire freezes, forming a highly unstable substance. (The Plane Below p.15)

Terrain Powers:
  Unstable Thaw
    Trigger: A fire power is used within 3 squares
    Effect: The frozen fire melts, releasing energy: burst 2, 2d6 fire and cold damage (DC 15 Reflex for half). The substance is destroyed.
  Thaw Burst
    Trigger: A fire attack targets a square of frozen fire
    Effect: The frozen fire thaws violently. Burst 2; each creature takes 2d6 fire and cold damage. The squares become difficult terrain (melted slush) for the rest of the encounter.

Mini Figures:
  Bloodweb Spider Swarm (×1) → Giant Spider (×1 of 6 available)
  Flame Snake (×1) → Mezlcoatl (×1 of 1 available)
  Dire Wolf (×1) → Wolf Casual (×1 of 6 available)
  Carrion Crawler (×1) → Jimmy Hollowhead (×1 of 1 available)

Terrain: Caught Breath
Tinkling crystals of warm breath frozen in air. Fragile and beautiful, but they shatter into cutting shards when disturbed. (Underdark p.120)

Terrain Powers:
  Breath Shatter
    Trigger: A creature moves through caught breath crystals
    Effect: Burst 1 around the square: 1d8 cold damage and the area becomes difficult terrain of sharp crystal fragments.
  Frozen Exhalation
    Trigger: A creature uses a close blast or close burst attack near caught breath crystals
    Effect: The attack's area is filled with suspended ice particles. The area becomes difficult terrain until the end of the next round, and creatures that enter take 5 cold damage.

Terrain: Choke Frost
Light, white mist that congeals into thick ice as creatures move through it, slowing and immobilizing. (DMG p.67)

Terrain Powers:
  Frost Snap
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: Choke frost congeals. Each creature in a frost square is slowed (save ends). A creature already slowed is immobilized instead (save ends).
  Ice Tomb
    Trigger: A creature ends its turn immobilized in choke frost
    Effect: The creature is restrained (save ends). On a failed save, the creature is instead stunned (save ends).
    Recharge: Single-use per creature per encounter

A strange, viscous mass pulsating with the energy of the Elemental Chaos — fire, cold, thunder, lightning, or a combination. (Manual of the Planes p.22)

Terrain Powers:
  Random Energy Burst
    Trigger: Initiative count 5

  Chaotic Mutation

    Effect: The creature gains one random benefit until the end of the encounter: resist 5 fire, +1 speed, or +2 to Fortitude. The GM rolls 1d6 to determine the effect.

    Trigger: A fire attack targets a square of frozen fire
    Effect: The frozen fire thaws violently. Burst 2; each creature takes 2d6 fire and cold damage. The squares become difficult terrain (melted slush) for the rest of the encounter.

    Effect: The chains wrap tighter. The creature is restrained (save ends) instead of slowed. While restrained, each attempt to teleport requires a saving throw; on failure, the teleport fails.

Terrain: Liquid Thunder
Highly volatile liquid pools coalesced from the force of a powerful elemental storm. Detonates on impact. (The Plane Below p.16)

Terrain Powers:
  Thunder Detonation
    Trigger: A creature enters a liquid thunder pool or the pool is struck
    Effect: Burst 2: 2d8 thunder damage (DC 15 Reflex for half) and each creature is deafened (save ends). The pool is consumed.
  Thunder Wave
    Trigger: A creature disturbs a pool of liquid thunder (entering or attacking in it)
    Effect: A concussive wave radiates outward. Each creature within 3 squares must succeed on a Fortitude save or be deafened and take a −2 penalty to attack rolls until the end of its next turn.

Terrain: Abandoned Temple
Desecrated altars and shattered statues line the nave. Dark energy pools in the apse.

Terrain Powers:
  Dark Energy Pulse
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: Necrotic energy surges from the desecrated altar. Each living creature within burst 3 of the altar takes 1d8 necrotic damage. Each undead creature in the area gains +1 to attack rolls until end of next round.
  Shattering Statue
    Trigger: A creature is pushed or slid into a statue
    Effect: The statue shatters. The creature takes 1d10 damage and each creature in burst 1 takes 1d6 damage from fragments. The square becomes difficult terrain.

Terrain: Ruined Pillars
Broken columns and scattered masonry create cover and difficult terrain.

Terrain Powers:
  Collapsing Pillar
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: One pillar (GM's choice) collapses. Each creature adjacent to it must succeed on a DC 15 Reflex save or take 1d10 damage and be knocked prone. The pillar's square becomes difficult terrain providing superior cover.
  Shifting Rubble
    Trigger: A creature uses a push, pull, or slide effect
    Effect: The target is pushed 1 additional square as loose stone shifts underfoot.

Terrain: Godsblood
The divine energy remaining within this godsblood temporarily supercharges natural healing, but at the cost of stamina and sanity. (Underdark p.102)

Terrain Powers:
  Divine Healing Surge
    Trigger: A creature drinks from or touches the godsblood
    Effect: The creature regains hit points as if spending two healing surges but loses one healing surge and is dazed until end of next turn.
  Blood Offering
    Trigger: A bloodied creature touches godsblood as a minor action
    Effect: The creature may spend a healing surge but heals only half the normal amount. In exchange, the creature gains +2 to attack rolls until the end of its next turn. The godsblood in that square is consumed.
    Recharge: Single-use per square

Terrain: Angelic Lantern
Small, pulsating balls of light that are faint manifestations of divine will. They illuminate and may aid the righteous. (Manual of the Planes p.21)

Terrain Powers:
  Guiding Light
    Trigger: A creature starts its turn within 2 squares of a lantern
    Effect: The creature gains +1 to attack rolls against undead and fiends until end of its turn.
  Blinding Radiance
    Trigger: A creature attacks an ally of the lantern's creator while within the lantern's light
    Effect: The lantern flares. The attacker takes 5 radiant damage and is blinded until the end of its next turn. Undead take 10 radiant damage instead.
    Recharge: Recharges after one round without triggering

Terrain: Frozen Lake
Cracked ice over dark water. The surface is slippery and sections may break under weight.

Terrain Powers:
  Cracking Ice
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: A 3×3 square section cracks. Creatures in the area must succeed on a DC 14 Acrobatics check or fall prone. On the next round, the section breaks — creatures still there fall into freezing water (2d6 cold damage, restrained until escape DC 15).
    Recharge: Each section can only crack once
  Slippery Surface
    Trigger: A creature charges or runs on the ice
    Effect: The creature must succeed on a DC 12 Acrobatics check or slide 1d4 squares in its direction of movement past its target.

  Geysir Backlash
    Trigger: A creature attempts to block or plug an elemental spout
    Effect: The spout builds pressure and erupts violently. Burst 1 centered on the spout; each creature takes 2d8 damage of the spout's element type and is pushed 3 squares. The spout is active for 1 additional round.

A campsite on a rocky ledge. One side drops away steeply — forced movement is deadly.

    Effect: DC 15 Acrobatics or the creature goes over. It can grab the edge (DC 12 Athletics) or fall, taking 5d10 damage.

    Effect: DC 14 Acrobatics or the creature falls 1d4 × 10 feet, taking falling damage.
  Canyon Ambush
    Trigger: A creature on high ground in the badlands attacks a creature below
    Effect: The attacker gains +2 to the attack roll and can push the target 1 square as rocks are dislodged by the attack. If the target is pushed into a wall, it takes 5 extra damage.

Terrain: Poison Swamp
Murky water, venomous reeds, and toxic gas pockets make every step dangerous.

Terrain Powers:

    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: A gas pocket erupts in burst 2 (GM places). Creatures in the area take 1d8 poison damage and are weakened until end of next turn (DC 15 Fortitude negates the weakened condition).

    Effect: The creature must spend 1 extra square of movement to leave its current square. If it cannot, it is immobilized until the end of its turn.

    Trigger: A creature moves more than 3 squares through the mist on its turn
    Effect: The creature takes 2d6 lightning damage and each creature adjacent to it takes 1d6 lightning damage.
  Static Chain
    Trigger: Two or more creatures wearing metal armor are within the mist
    Effect: Lightning arcs between them. Each armored creature takes 1d8 lightning damage and the space between them becomes charged until the end of the round, dealing 5 lightning damage to anyone crossing it.

    Trigger: Initiative count 5

    Effect: Burst 2: 2d8 thunder damage (DC 15 Reflex for half) and each creature is deafened (save ends). The pool is consumed.
  Thunder Wave
    Trigger: A creature disturbs a pool of liquid thunder (entering or attacking in it)
    Effect: A concussive wave radiates outward. Each creature within 3 squares must succeed on a Fortitude save or be deafened and take a −2 penalty to attack rolls until the end of its next turn.

  Reflection Trap
    Trigger: A ranged or area attack passes through a square of mirror crystal
    Effect: The attacker rerolls the attack against one ally of their choice within 3 squares of the crystal, using the same attack roll. The ally becomes the secondary target.

Small, pulsating balls of light that are faint manifestations of divine will. They illuminate and may aid the righteous. (Manual of the Planes p.21)

Terrain Powers:
  Guiding Light
    Trigger: A creature starts its turn within 2 squares of a lantern
    Effect: The creature gains +1 to attack rolls against undead and fiends until end of its turn.
  Blinding Radiance
    Trigger: A creature attacks an ally of the lantern's creator while within the lantern's light
    Effect: The lantern flares. The attacker takes 5 radiant damage and is blinded until the end of its next turn. Undead take 10 radiant damage instead.
    Recharge: Recharges after one round without triggering

Terrain: Energy Node
A particularly strong confluence of arcane, divine, or other forms of power. Boosts attacks or healing for those standing within. (DMG2 p.59)

Terrain Powers:
  Energy Surge
    Trigger: A creature uses a power with a damage roll while in the node
    Effect: Add 1d6 extra damage of a random type (roll 1d4: 1=fire, 2=cold, 3=lightning, 4=thunder).
  Node Overcharge
    Trigger: Three or more creatures occupy squares adjacent to the energy node simultaneously
    Effect: The node discharges. Close burst 3; each creature in the burst takes 2d8 force damage and is pushed 2 squares. The node becomes inert for 1 round.

  Bugbear Strangler (Goblin) (×1) → Goblin Scoundrel (×1 of 2 available)

Terrain: Shifting Maze
Walls rearrange themselves between rounds. Nothing stays where you last saw it.

Terrain Powers:
  Walls Rearrange
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: The maze shifts. Each creature must succeed on DC 14 Perception or lose line of sight to all other creatures until the end of its next turn. The GM may move one wall section up to 3 squares.
  False Passage
    Trigger: A creature moves through a doorway or opening
    Effect: Roll 1d6. On a 1-2, the doorway leads somewhere unexpected — the creature appears in a random opening within 6 squares instead.

Terrain: Goblin Warrens
Cramped tunnels with pit traps, narrow choke points, and ambush alcoves.

Terrain Powers:
  Hidden Pit Trap
    Trigger: A creature enters a trapped square (GM places 1d4 traps at setup)
    Effect: The floor gives way. The creature takes 1d10 damage and falls prone in a 10-foot pit (DC 15 Athletics to climb out).
  Tunnel Ambush
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: A narrow tunnel (GM's choice) collapses to 1 square wide until cleared (DC 15 Athletics, standard action). Creatures inside take 1d6 damage.

Minotaur Warrior      Soldier       Standard    10    1  Monster Manual 1 p.190
Dragonborn Gladiator  Soldier       Standard    10    1  Monster Manual 1 p.86
Carrion Crawler       Controller    Standard     7    1  Monster Manual 1 p.40
Tiefling Heretic      Artillery     Standard     6    1  Monster Manual 1 p.250

Mini Figures:
  Minotaur Warrior (×1) → Oni Brute (×1 of 1 available)
  Dragonborn Gladiator (×1) → Usgrit Dragonslayer (×1 of 1 available)
  Carrion Crawler (×1) → Barghest (×1 of 1 available)
  Tiefling Heretic (×1) → Odi’ram, Lord of Rage (×1 of 1 available)

Terrain: Acid Cavern
Pools of caustic liquid dot the floor, and stalactites drip corrosive slime.

Terrain Powers:
  Acid Geyser
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: An acid pool erupts in a burst 1 (GM places). Creatures in the area take 2d6 acid damage (DC 14 Reflex for half). The area becomes an acid zone until end of next round (entering or starting turn: 1d6 acid).
  Dripping Stalactite
    Trigger: A creature takes acid damage
    Effect: A caustic stalactite above destabilizes. At the start of the creature's next turn, it drips acid on the creature for 1d6 acid damage (DC 12 Reflex negates).

Terrain: Mirror Crystal
Crystals that cause strange twists and turns in space — teleportation becomes unpredictable. (DMG p.68)

Terrain Powers:
  Spatial Twist
    Trigger: A creature uses a teleport power within 3 squares of a mirror crystal
    Effect: Roll 1d6. On 1-3, the destination shifts 1d4 squares in a random direction. On 4-6, the teleport works normally.
  Reflection Trap
    Trigger: A ranged or area attack passes through a square of mirror crystal
    Effect: The attacker rerolls the attack against one ally of their choice within 3 squares of the crystal, using the same attack roll. The ally becomes the secondary target.

  Sahuagin Priest (×1) → Octus (×1 of 3 available)

Terrain: Whirlwind
Whirlwinds form where elemental energy is strong. They slide creatures around and disrupt ranged attacks. (DMG p.69)

Terrain Powers:
  Gust Blast
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: The whirlwind slides each creature within burst 2 of its center 1d4 squares in a random direction. Ranged attacks through the whirlwind take a −2 penalty until end of next round.
  Vortex Lift
    Trigger: A creature enters the whirlwind's space
    Effect: The creature is lifted 4 squares into the air and suspended (save ends). While suspended, the creature cannot move except to shift, and falls when the effect ends.

Terrain: Chainstorm
Phantasmal, shrieking chains — thought to be echoes of an imprisoned deity's rage manifesting in the Astral Sea. (The Plane Above p.29)

Terrain Powers:
  Phantom Chain Lash
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: Each creature in the area: +12 vs. Reflex, 1d10 damage and the creature is immobilized until end of its next turn.
  Chain Binding
    Trigger: A creature is hit by a phantom chain lash and is already slowed
    Effect: The chains wrap tighter. The creature is restrained (save ends) instead of slowed. While restrained, each attempt to teleport requires a saving throw; on failure, the teleport fails.

Mini Figures:
  Blade Spider (×1) → Ravenous Bulette (×1 of 2 available)
  Flame Snake (×1) → Bone Naga (×1 of 1 available)
  Scytheclaw Drake (×1) → Turill (×1 of 1 available)
  Foulspawn Mockery (×1) → Nothail (×1 of 1 available)

Terrain: Liquid Thunder
Highly volatile liquid pools coalesced from the force of a powerful elemental storm. Detonates on impact. (The Plane Below p.16)

Terrain Powers:
  Thunder Detonation
    Trigger: A creature enters a liquid thunder pool or the pool is struck
    Effect: Burst 2: 2d8 thunder damage (DC 15 Reflex for half) and each creature is deafened (save ends). The pool is consumed.
  Thunder Wave
    Trigger: A creature disturbs a pool of liquid thunder (entering or attacking in it)
    Effect: A concussive wave radiates outward. Each creature within 3 squares must succeed on a Fortitude save or be deafened and take a −2 penalty to attack rolls until the end of its next turn.

Terrain: Lightning Mist
Fields of gray-blue fog that drift slowly through the Elemental Chaos, hazardous to traverse quickly — crackling with static. (The Plane Below p.16)

Terrain Powers:
  Static Discharge
    Trigger: A creature moves more than 3 squares through the mist on its turn
    Effect: The creature takes 2d6 lightning damage and each creature adjacent to it takes 1d6 lightning damage.
  Static Chain
    Trigger: Two or more creatures wearing metal armor are within the mist
    Effect: Lightning arcs between them. Each armored creature takes 1d8 lightning damage and the space between them becomes charged until the end of the round, dealing 5 lightning damage to anyone crossing it.

  Geysir Backlash
    Trigger: A creature attempts to block or plug an elemental spout
    Effect: The spout builds pressure and erupts violently. Burst 1 centered on the spout; each creature takes 2d8 damage of the spout's element type and is pushed 3 squares. The spout is active for 1 additional round.

  Shattering Statue
    Trigger: A creature is pushed or slid into a statue
    Effect: The statue shatters. The creature takes 1d10 damage and each creature in burst 1 takes 1d6 damage from fragments. The square becomes difficult terrain.

  Hidden Pit Trap
    Trigger: A creature enters a trapped square (GM places 1d4 traps at setup)
    Effect: The floor gives way. The creature takes 1d10 damage and falls prone in a 10-foot pit (DC 15 Athletics to climb out).
  Tunnel Ambush
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: A narrow tunnel (GM's choice) collapses to 1 square wide until cleared (DC 15 Athletics, standard action). Creatures inside take 1d6 damage.

Terrain: Shifting Maze
Walls rearrange themselves between rounds. Nothing stays where you last saw it.

Terrain Powers:
  Walls Rearrange
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: The maze shifts. Each creature must succeed on DC 14 Perception or lose line of sight to all other creatures until the end of its next turn. The GM may move one wall section up to 3 squares.
  False Passage
    Trigger: A creature moves through a doorway or opening
    Effect: Roll 1d6. On a 1-2, the doorway leads somewhere unexpected — the creature appears in a random opening within 6 squares instead.

Terrain: Thunder Shards
Crystalline shards of solidified thunder energy. Unstable and dangerous — they detonate when disturbed. (DMG2 p.61)

Terrain Powers:
  Shard Detonation
    Trigger: A creature moves into a thunder shard square
    Effect: The shard detonates: burst 1, 2d6 thunder damage (DC 14 Reflex for half). Each creature damaged is also deafened (save ends). The shard is destroyed.
  Resonant Chain
    Trigger: A thunder shard detonates
    Effect: The sound wave triggers adjacent shards. Each creature within 2 squares of the chain takes 1d8 thunder damage and is knocked prone. All triggered shards are destroyed.

Terrain: Lightning Mist
Fields of gray-blue fog that drift slowly through the Elemental Chaos, hazardous to traverse quickly — crackling with static. (The Plane Below p.16)

Terrain Powers:
  Static Discharge
    Trigger: A creature moves more than 3 squares through the mist on its turn
    Effect: The creature takes 2d6 lightning damage and each creature adjacent to it takes 1d6 lightning damage.
  Static Chain
    Trigger: Two or more creatures wearing metal armor are within the mist
    Effect: Lightning arcs between them. Each armored creature takes 1d8 lightning damage and the space between them becomes charged until the end of the round, dealing 5 lightning damage to anyone crossing it.

    Effect: Burst 2: 2d8 thunder damage (DC 15 Reflex for half) and each creature is deafened (save ends). The pool is consumed.

  Geysir Backlash
    Trigger: A creature attempts to block or plug an elemental spout
    Effect: The spout builds pressure and erupts violently. Burst 1 centered on the spout; each creature takes 2d8 damage of the spout's element type and is pushed 3 squares. The spout is active for 1 additional round.

    Effect: Roll 1d6. On 1-3, the destination shifts 1d4 squares in a random direction. On 4-6, the teleport works normally.

    Trigger: A creature is reduced to bloodied in the cavern
    Effect: The lichen rapidly spreads to cover the creature's square and adjacent squares, creating difficult terrain. Each creature starting its turn in these squares takes 5 necrotic damage.
    Recharge: Recharges when no bloodied creature is in the cavern

Terrain: Hungry Void
Some places in the unending night below sap existence and unravel the threads of life. Creatures nearby feel their vitality drain. (Underdark p.120)

Terrain Powers:
  Void Drain
    Trigger: A creature starts its turn in the hungry void
    Effect: The creature loses a healing surge. If it has no healing surges, it takes 1d10 necrotic damage instead.
  Void Collapse
    Trigger: A creature is reduced to 0 hit points within the hungry void
    Effect: The void surges hungrily. Each creature within 2 squares is pulled 1 square toward the fallen creature's space. The space becomes heavily obscured for 1 round.

~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

  Kuo-Toa Marauder (×1) → Male Merrow (×1 of 1 available)
  Gibbering Mouther (×1) → Nothail (×1 of 1 available)

Terrain: Poison Swamp
Murky water, venomous reeds, and toxic gas pockets make every step dangerous.

Terrain Powers:
  Toxic Gas Pocket
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: A gas pocket erupts in burst 2 (GM places). Creatures in the area take 1d8 poison damage and are weakened until end of next turn (DC 15 Fortitude negates the weakened condition).
  Sucking Mud
    Trigger: A creature starts its turn in swamp water
    Effect: The creature must spend 1 extra square of movement to leave its current square. If it cannot, it is immobilized until the end of its turn.

  Chain Binding
    Trigger: A creature is hit by a phantom chain lash and is already slowed
    Effect: The chains wrap tighter. The creature is restrained (save ends) instead of slowed. While restrained, each attempt to teleport requires a saving throw; on failure, the teleport fails.

  Winter Wolf (×1) → White Wolf (×1 of 1 available)
  Dragonborn Fire Adept (×1) → Archangel Azazel (×1 of 1 available)

Terrain: Godrock
Where fragments of Torog's bones struck the earth, formations called godrock sprouted. They radiate lingering divine agony. (Underdark p.119)

Terrain Powers:
  Agony Pulse
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: Each creature within burst 2 of the godrock takes 1d6 psychic damage and is slowed until end of its next turn (DC 15 Will negates the slowed condition).
  Echo of Agony
    Trigger: A creature is reduced to bloodied while touching godrock
    Effect: The godrock channels Torog's suffering. Each creature within 3 squares takes 1d8 psychic damage and is immobilized until the end of its next turn.

Terrain: Godsblood
The divine energy remaining within this godsblood temporarily supercharges natural healing, but at the cost of stamina and sanity. (Underdark p.102)

Terrain Powers:
  Divine Healing Surge
    Trigger: A creature drinks from or touches the godsblood
    Effect: The creature regains hit points as if spending two healing surges but loses one healing surge and is dazed until end of next turn.
  Blood Offering
    Trigger: A bloodied creature touches godsblood as a minor action
    Effect: The creature may spend a healing surge but heals only half the normal amount. In exchange, the creature gains +2 to attack rolls until the end of its next turn. The godsblood in that square is consumed.
    Recharge: Single-use per square

  Shadar-Kai Gloom Lord (×1) → Erosius, Lord of Passion (×1 of 1 available)
  Deva Zealot (×1) → Solar Prisoner (×1 of 1 available)
  Dragonborn Fire Adept (×1) → Archangel Azazel (×1 of 1 available)

Terrain: Shifting Maze
Walls rearrange themselves between rounds. Nothing stays where you last saw it.

Terrain Powers:
  Walls Rearrange
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: The maze shifts. Each creature must succeed on DC 14 Perception or lose line of sight to all other creatures until the end of its next turn. The GM may move one wall section up to 3 squares.
  False Passage
    Trigger: A creature moves through a doorway or opening
    Effect: Roll 1d6. On a 1-2, the doorway leads somewhere unexpected — the creature appears in a random opening within 6 squares instead.

Terrain: Goblin Warrens
Cramped tunnels with pit traps, narrow choke points, and ambush alcoves.

Terrain Powers:
  Hidden Pit Trap
    Trigger: A creature enters a trapped square (GM places 1d4 traps at setup)
    Effect: The floor gives way. The creature takes 1d10 damage and falls prone in a 10-foot pit (DC 15 Athletics to climb out).
  Tunnel Ambush
    Trigger: Initiative count 5
    Effect: A narrow tunnel (GM's choice) collapses to 1 square wide until cleared (DC 15 Athletics, standard action). Creatures inside take 1d6 damage.

Organic sludge that forms where life force rises up through the Feydark into the Feywild. Teeming with nascent life. (Underdark p.101)

    Effect: Tiny creatures sprout and attack: the prone creature takes 1d8 damage and is dazed until it stands. If the creature is bloodied, it regains 5 HP as the mud's primal energy responds to weakness.

    Effect: The creature regains hit points equal to its level. Undead and constructs instead sink deeper — they are slowed (save ends).

  Sympathetic Resonance
    Trigger: A doomlight crystal detonates
    Effect: Each other doomlight crystal within 5 squares pulses. Creatures adjacent to any pulsing crystal take 1d6 radiant damage and are dazed until the end of their next turn.
